09001001  janamejaya uvAca
09001001a evaM nipAtite karNe samare savyasAcinA
09001001c alpAvaziSTAH kuravaH kim akurvata vai dvija
09001002a udIryamANaM ca balaM dRSTvA rAjA suyodhanaH
09001002c pANDavaiH prAptakAlaM ca kiM prApadyata kauravaH
09001003a etad icchAmy ahaM zrotuM tad AcakSva dvijottama
09001003c na hi tRpyAmi pUrveSAM zRNvAnaz caritaM mahat
09001004  vaizaMpAyana uvAca
09001004a tataH karNe hate rAjan dhArtarASTraH suyodhanaH
09001004c bhRzaM zokArNave magno nirAzaH sarvato 'bhavat
09001005a hA karNa hA karNa iti zocamAnaH punaH punaH
09001005c kRcchrAt svazibiraM prAyAd dhatazeSair nRpaiH saha
09001006a sa samAzvAsyamAno 'pi hetubhiH zAstranizcitaiH
09001006c rAjabhir nAlabhac charma sUtaputravadhaM smaran
09001007a sa daivaM balavan matvA bhavitavyaM ca pArthivaH
09001007c saMgrAme nizcayaM kRtvA punar yuddhAya niryayau
09001008a zalyaM senApatiM kRtvA vidhivad rAjapuMgavaH
09001008c raNAya niryayau rAjA hatazeSair nRpaiH saha
09001009a tataH sutumulaM yuddhaM kurupANDavasenayoH
09001009c babhUva bharatazreSTha devAsuraraNopamam
09001010a tataH zalyo mahArAja kRtvA kadanam Ahave
09001010c pANDusainyasya madhyAhne dharmarAjena pAtitaH
09001011a tato duryodhano rAjA hatabandhU raNAjirAt
09001011c apasRtya hradaM ghoraM viveza ripujAd bhayAt
09001012a athAparAhNe tasyAhnaH parivArya mahArathaiH
09001012c hradAd AhUya yogena bhImasenena pAtitaH
09001013a tasmin hate maheSvAse hataziSTAs trayo rathAH
09001013c saMrabhAn nizi rAjendra jaghnuH pAJcAlasainikAn
09001014a tataH pUrvAhNasamaye zibirAd etya saMjayaH
09001014c praviveza purIM dIno duHkhazokasamanvitaH
09001015a pravizya ca puraM tUrNaM bhujAv ucchritya duHkhitaH
09001015c vepamAnas tato rAjJaH praviveza nivezanam
09001016a ruroda ca naravyAghra hA rAjann iti duHkhitaH
09001016c aho bata vivignAH sma nidhanena mahAtmanaH
09001017a aho subalavAn kAlo gatiz ca paramA tathA
09001017c zakratulyabalAH sarve yatrAvadhyanta pArthivAH
09001018a dRSTvaiva ca puro rAjaJ janaH sarvaH sa saMjayam
09001018c praruroda bhRzodvigno hA rAjann iti sasvaram
09001019a AkumAraM naravyAghra tat puraM vai samantataH
09001019c ArtanAdaM mahac cakre zrutvA vinihataM nRpam
09001020a dhAvataz cApy apazyac ca tatra trIn puruSarSabhAn
09001020c naSTacittAn ivonmattAJ zokena bhRzapIDitAn
09001021a tathA sa vihvalaH sUtaH pravizya nRpatikSayam
09001021c dadarza nRpatizreSThaM prajJAcakSuSam Izvaram
09001022a dRSTvA cAsInam anaghaM samantAt parivAritam
09001022c snuSAbhir bharatazreSTha gAndhAryA vidureNa ca
09001023a tathAnyaiz ca suhRdbhiz ca jJAtibhiz ca hitaiSibhiH
09001023c tam eva cArthaM dhyAyantaM karNasya nidhanaM prati
09001024a rudann evAbravId vAkyaM rAjAnaM janamejaya
09001024c nAtihRSTamanAH sUto bASpasaMdigdhayA girA
09001025a saMjayo 'haM naravyAghra namas te bharatarSabha
09001025c madrAdhipo hataH zalyaH zakuniH saubalas tathA
09001025e ulUkaH puruSavyAghra kaitavyo dRDhavikramaH
09001026a saMzaptakA hatAH sarve kAmbojAz ca zakaiH saha
09001026c mlecchAz ca pArvatIyAz ca yavanAz ca nipAtitAH
09001027a prAcyA hatA mahArAja dAkSiNAtyAz ca sarvazaH
09001027c udIcyA nihatAH sarve pratIcyAz ca narAdhipa
09001027e rAjAno rAjaputrAz ca sarvato nihatA nRpa
09001028a duryodhano hato rAjan yathoktaM pANDavena ca
09001028c bhagnasaktho mahArAja zete pAMsuSu rUSitaH
09001029a dhRSTadyumno hato rAjaJ zikhaNDI cAparAjitaH
09001029c uttamaujA yudhAmanyus tathA rAjan prabhadrakAH
09001030a pAJcAlAz ca naravyAghrAz cedayaz ca niSUditAH
09001030c tava putrA hatAH sarve draupadeyAz ca bhArata
09001030e karNaputro hataH zUro vRSaseno mahAbalaH
09001031a narA vinihatAH sarve gajAz ca vinipAtitAH
09001031c rathinaz ca naravyAghra hayAz ca nihatA yudhi
09001032a kiMciccheSaM ca zibiraM tAvakAnAM kRtaM vibho
09001032c pANDavAnAM ca zUrANAM samAsAdya parasparam
09001033a prAyaH strIzeSam abhavaj jagat kAlena mohitam
09001033c sapta pANDavataH zeSA dhArtarASTrAs tathA trayaH
09001034a te caiva bhrAtaraH paJca vAsudevo 'tha sAtyakiH
09001034c kRpaz ca kRtavarmA ca drauNiz ca jayatAM varaH
09001035a tavApy ete mahArAja rathino nRpasattama
09001035c akSauhiNInAM sarvAsAM sametAnAM janezvara
09001035e ete zeSA mahArAja sarve 'nye nidhanaM gatAH
09001036a kAlena nihataM sarvaM jagad vai bharatarSabha
09001036c duryodhanaM vai purataH kRtvA vairasya bhArata
09001037a etac chrutvA vacaH krUraM dhRtarASTro janezvaraH
09001037c nipapAta mahArAja gatasattvo mahItale
09001038a tasmin nipatite bhUmau viduro 'pi mahAyazAH
09001038c nipapAta mahArAja rAjavyasanakarzitaH
09001039a gAndhArI ca nRpazreSTha sarvAz ca kuruyoSitaH
09001039c patitAH sahasA bhUmau zrutvA krUraM vacaz ca tAH
09001040a niHsaMjJaM patitaM bhUmau tadAsId rAjamaNDalam
09001040c pralApayuktA mahatI kathA nyastA paTe yathA
09001041a kRcchreNa tu tato rAjA dhRtarASTro mahIpatiH
09001041c zanair alabhata prANAn putravyasanakarzitaH
09001042a labdhvA tu sa nRpaH saMjJAM vepamAnaH suduHkhitaH
09001042c udIkSya ca dizaH sarvAH kSattAraM vAkyam abravIt
09001043a vidvan kSattar mahAprAjJa tvaM gatir bharatarSabha
09001043c mamAnAthasya subhRzaM putrair hInasya sarvazaH
09001043e evam uktvA tato bhUyo visaMjJo nipapAta ha
09001044a taM tathA patitaM dRSTvA bAndhavA ye 'sya ke cana
09001044c zItais tu siSicus toyair vivyajur vyajanair api
09001045a sa tu dIrgheNa kAlena pratyAzvasto mahIpatiH
09001045c tUSNIM dadhyau mahIpAlaH putravyasanakarzitaH
09001045e niHzvasaJ jihmaga iva kumbhakSipto vizAM pate
09001046a saMjayo 'py arudat tatra dRSTvA rAjAnam Aturam
09001046c tathA sarvAH striyaz caiva gAndhArI ca yazasvinI
09001047a tato dIrgheNa kAlena viduraM vAkyam abravIt
09001047c dhRtarASTro naravyAghro muhyamAno muhur muhuH
09001048a gacchantu yoSitaH sarvA gAndhArI ca yazasvinI
09001048c tatheme suhRdaH sarve bhrazyate me mano bhRzam
09001049a evam uktas tataH kSattA tAH striyo bharatarSabha
09001049c visarjayAm Asa zanair vepamAnaH punaH punaH
09001050a nizcakramus tataH sarvAs tAH striyo bharatarSabha
09001050c suhRdaz ca tataH sarve dRSTvA rAjAnam Aturam
09001051a tato narapatiM tatra labdhasaMjJaM paraMtapa
09001051c avekSya saMjayo dIno rodamAnaM bhRzAturam
09001052a prAJjalir niHzvasantaM ca taM narendraM muhur muhuH
09001052c samAzvAsayata kSattA vacasA madhureNa ca
09002001  vaizaMpAyana uvAca
09002001a visRSTAsv atha nArISu dhRtarASTro 'mbikAsutaH
09002001c vilalApa mahArAja duHkhAd duHkhataraM gataH
09002002a sadhUmam iva niHzvasya karau dhunvan punaH punaH
09002002c vicintya ca mahArAja tato vacanam abravIt
09002003a aho bata mahad duHkhaM yad ahaM pANDavAn raNe
09002003c kSemiNaz cAvyayAMz caiva tvattaH sUta zRNomi vai
09002004a vajrasAramayaM nUnaM hRdayaM sudRDhaM mama
09002004c yac chrutvA nihatAn putrAn dIryate na sahasradhA
09002005a cintayitvA vacas teSAM bAlakrIDAM ca saMjaya
09002005c adya zrutvA hatAn putrAn bhRzaM me dIryate manaH
09002006a andhatvAd yadi teSAM tu na me rUpanidarzanam
09002006c putrasnehakRtA prItir nityam eteSu dhAritA
09002007a bAlabhAvam atikrAntAn yauvanasthAMz ca tAn aham
09002007c madhyaprAptAMs tathA zrutvA hRSTa AsaM tathAnagha
09002008a tAn adya nihatAJ zrutvA hRtaizvaryAn hRtaujasaH
09002008c na labhe vai kva cic chAntiM putrAdhibhir abhiplutaH
09002009a ehy ehi putra rAjendra mamAnAthasya sAMpratam
09002009c tvayA hIno mahAbAho kAM nu yAsyAmy ahaM gatim
09002010a gatir bhUtvA mahArAja jJAtInAM suhRdAM tathA
09002010c andhaM vRddhaM ca mAM vIra vihAya kva nu gacchasi
09002011a sA kRpA sA ca te prItiH sA ca rAjan sumAnitA
09002011c kathaM vinihataH pArthaiH saMyugeSv aparAjitaH
09002012a kathaM tvaM pRthivIpAlAn bhuktvA tAta samAgatAn
09002012c zeSe vinihato bhUmau prAkRtaH kunRpo yathA
09002013a ko nu mAm utthitaM kAlye tAta tAteti vakSyati
09002013c mahArAjeti satataM lokanAtheti cAsakRt
09002014a pariSvajya ca mAM kaNThe snehenAklinnalocanaH
09002014c anuzAdhIti kauravya tat sAdhu vada me vacaH
09002015a nanu nAmAham azrauSaM vacanaM tava putraka
09002015c bhUyasI mama pRthvIyaM yathA pArthasya no tathA
09002016a bhagadattaH kRpaH zalya Avantyo 'tha jayadrathaH
09002016c bhUrizravAH somadatto mahArAjo 'tha bAhlikaH
09002017a azvatthAmA ca bhojaz ca mAgadhaz ca mahAbalaH
09002017c bRhadbalaz ca kAzIzaH zakuniz cApi saubalaH
09002018a mlecchAz ca bahusAhasrAH zakAz ca yavanaiH saha
09002018c sudakSiNaz ca kAmbojas trigartAdhipatis tathA
09002019a bhISmaH pitAmahaz caiva bhAradvAjo 'tha gautamaH
09002019c zrutAyuz cAcyutAyuz ca zatAyuz cApi vIryavAn
09002020a jalasaMdho 'thArzyazRGgI rAkSasaz cApy alAyudhaH
09002020c alaMbuso mahAbAhuH subAhuz ca mahArathaH
09002021a ete cAnye ca bahavo rAjAno rAjasattama
09002021c madartham udyatAH sarve prANAMs tyaktvA raNe prabho
09002022a yeSAM madhye sthito yuddhe bhrAtRbhiH parivAritaH
09002022c yodhayiSyAmy ahaM pArthAn pAJcAlAMz caiva sarvazaH
09002023a cedIMz ca nRpazArdUla draupadeyAMz ca saMyuge
09002023c sAtyakiM kuntibhojaM ca rAkSasaM ca ghaTotkacam
09002024a eko 'py eSAM mahArAja samarthaH saMnivAraNe
09002024c samare pANDaveyAnAM saMkruddho hy abhidhAvatAm
09002024e kiM punaH sahitA vIrAH kRtavairAz ca pANDavaiH
09002025a atha vA sarva evaite pANDavasyAnuyAyibhiH
09002025c yotsyanti saha rAjendra haniSyanti ca tAn mRdhe
09002026a karNas tv eko mayA sArdhaM nihaniSyati pANDavAn
09002026c tato nRpatayo vIrAH sthAsyanti mama zAsane
09002027a yaz ca teSAM praNetA vai vAsudevo mahAbalaH
09002027c na sa saMnahyate rAjann iti mAm abravId vacaH
09002028a tasyAhaM vadataH sUta bahuzo mama saMnidhau
09002028c yuktito hy anupazyAmi nihatAn pANDavAn mRdhe
09002029a teSAM madhye sthitA yatra hanyante mama putrakAH
09002029c vyAyacchamAnAH samare kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002030a bhISmaz ca nihato yatra lokanAthaH pratApavAn
09002030c zikhaNDinaM samAsAdya mRgendra iva jambukam
09002031a droNaz ca brAhmaNo yatra sarvazastrAstrapAragaH
09002031c nihataH pANDavaiH saMkhye kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002032a bhUrizravA hato yatra somadattaz ca saMyuge
09002032c bAhlIkaz ca mahArAja kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002033a sudakSiNo hato yatra jalasaMdhaz ca kauravaH
09002033c zrutAyuz cAcyutAyuz ca kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002034a bRhadbalo hato yatra mAgadhaz ca mahAbalaH
09002034c Avantyo nihato yatra trigartaz ca janAdhipaH
09002034e saMzaptakAz ca bahavaH kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002035a alaMbusas tathA rAjan rAkSasaz cApy alAyudhaH
09002035c ArzyazRGgaz ca nihataH kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002036a nArAyaNA hatA yatra gopAlA yuddhadurmadAH
09002036c mlecchAz ca bahusAhasrAH kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002037a zakuniH saubalo yatra kaitavyaz ca mahAbalaH
09002037c nihataH sabalo vIraH kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002038a rAjAno rAjaputrAz ca zUrAH parighabAhavaH
09002038c nihatA bahavo yatra kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002039a nAnAdezasamAvRttAH kSatriyA yatra saMjaya
09002039c nihatAH samare sarve kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002040a putrAz ca me vinihatAH pautrAz caiva mahAbalAH
09002040c vayasyA bhrAtaraz caiva kim anyad bhAgadheyataH
09002041a bhAgadheyasamAyukto dhruvam utpadyate naraH
09002041c yaz ca bhAgyasamAyuktaH sa zubhaM prApnuyAn naraH
09002042a ahaM viyuktaH svair bhAgyaiH putraiz caiveha saMjaya
09002042c katham adya bhaviSyAmi vRddhaH zatruvazaM gataH
09002043a nAnyad atra paraM manye vanavAsAd Rte prabho
09002043c so 'haM vanaM gamiSyAmi nirbandhur jJAtisaMkSaye
09002044a na hi me 'nyad bhavec chreyo vanAbhyupagamAd Rte
09002044c imAm avasthAM prAptasya lUnapakSasya saMjaya
09002045a duryodhano hato yatra zalyaz ca nihato yudhi
09002045c duHzAsano vizastaz ca vikarNaz ca mahAbalaH
09002046a kathaM hi bhImasenasya zroSye 'haM zabdam uttamam
09002046c ekena samare yena hataM putrazataM mama
09002047a asakRd vadatas tasya duryodhanavadhena ca
09002047c duHkhazokAbhisaMtapto na zroSye paruSA giraH
09002048a evaM sa zokasaMtaptaH pArthivo hatabAndhavaH
09002048c muhur muhur muhyamAnaH putrAdhibhir abhiplutaH
09002049a vilapya suciraM kAlaM dhRtarASTro 'mbikAsutaH
09002049c dIrgham uSNaM ca niHzvasya cintayitvA parAbhavam
09002050a duHkhena mahatA rAjA saMtapto bharatarSabha
09002050c punar gAvalgaNiM sUtaM paryapRcchad yathAtatham
09002051a bhISmadroNau hatau zrutvA sUtaputraM ca pAtitam
09002051c senApatiM praNetAraM kim akurvata mAmakAH
09002052a yaM yaM senApraNetAraM yudhi kurvanti mAmakAH
09002052c acireNaiva kAlena taM taM nighnanti pANDavAH
09002053a raNamUrdhni hato bhISmaH pazyatAM vaH kirITinA
09002053c evam eva hato droNaH sarveSAm eva pazyatAm
09002054a evam eva hataH karNaH sUtaputraH pratApavAn
09002054c sa rAjakAnAM sarveSAM pazyatAM vaH kirITinA
09002055a pUrvam evAham ukto vai vidureNa mahAtmanA
09002055c duryodhanAparAdhena prajeyaM vinaziSyati
09002056a ke cin na samyak pazyanti mUDhAH samyak tathApare
09002056c tad idaM mama mUDhasya tathAbhUtaM vacaH sma ha
09002057a yad abravIn me dharmAtmA viduro dIrghadarzivAn
09002057c tat tathA samanuprAptaM vacanaM satyavAdinaH
09002058a daivopahatacittena yan mayApakRtaM purA
09002058c anayasya phalaM tasya brUhi gAvalgaNe punaH
09002059a ko vA mukham anIkAnAm AsIt karNe nipAtite
09002059c arjunaM vAsudevaM ca ko vA pratyudyayau rathI
09002060a ke 'rakSan dakSiNaM cakraM madrarAjasya saMyuge
09002060c vAmaM ca yoddhukAmasya ke vA vIrasya pRSThataH
09002061a kathaM ca vaH sametAnAM madrarAjo mahAbalaH
09002061c nihataH pANDavaiH saMkhye putro vA mama saMjaya
09002062a brUhi sarvaM yathAtattvaM bharatAnAM mahAkSayam
09002062c yathA ca nihataH saMkhye putro duryodhano mama
09002063a pAJcAlAz ca yathA sarve nihatAH sapadAnugAH
09002063c dhRSTadyumnaH zikhaNDI ca draupadyAH paJca cAtmajAH
09002064a pANDavAz ca yathA muktAs tathobhau sAtvatau yudhi
09002064c kRpaz ca kRtavarmA ca bhAradvAjasya cAtmajaH
09002065a yad yathA yAdRzaM caiva yuddhaM vRttaM ca sAMpratam
09002065c akhilaM zrotum icchAmi kuzalo hy asi saMjaya
09003001  saMjaya uvAca
09003001a zRNu rAjann avahito yathA vRtto mahAn kSayaH
09003001c kurUNAM pANDavAnAM ca samAsAdya parasparam
09003002a nihate sUtaputre tu pANDavena mahAtmanA
09003002c vidruteSu ca sainyeSu samAnIteSu cAsakRt
09003003a vimukhe tava putre tu zokopahatacetasi
09003003c bhRzodvigneSu sainyeSu dRSTvA pArthasya vikramam
09003004a dhyAyamAneSu sainyeSu duHkhaM prApteSu bhArata
09003004c balAnAM mathyamAnAnAM zrutvA ninadam uttamam
09003005a abhijJAnaM narendrANAM vikRtaM prekSya saMyuge
09003005c patitAn rathanIDAMz ca rathAMz cApi mahAtmanAm
09003006a raNe vinihatAn nAgAn dRSTvA pattIMz ca mAriSa
09003006c AyodhanaM cAtighoraM rudrasyAkrIDasaMnibham
09003007a aprakhyAtiM gatAnAM tu rAjJAM zatasahasrazaH
09003007c kRpAviSTaH kRpo rAjan vayaHzIlasamanvitaH
09003008a abravIt tatra tejasvI so 'bhisRtya janAdhipam
09003008c duryodhanaM manyuvazAd vacanaM vacanakSamaH
09003009a duryodhana nibodhedaM yat tvA vakSyAmi kaurava
09003009c zrutvA kuru mahArAja yadi te rocate 'nagha
09003010a na yuddhadharmAc chreyAn vai panthA rAjendra vidyate
09003010c yaM samAzritya yudhyante kSatriyAH kSatriyarSabha
09003011a putro bhrAtA pitA caiva svasreyo mAtulas tathA
09003011c saMbandhibAndhavAz caiva yodhyA vai kSatrajIvinA
09003012a vadhe caiva paro dharmas tathAdharmaH palAyane
09003012c te sma ghorAM samApannA jIvikAM jIvitArthinaH
09003013a tatra tvAM prativakSyAmi kiM cid eva hitaM vacaH
09003013c hate bhISme ca droNe ca karNe caiva mahArathe
09003014a jayadrathe ca nihate tava bhrAtRSu cAnagha
09003014c lakSmaNe tava putre ca kiM zeSaM paryupAsmahe
09003015a yeSu bhAraM samAsajya rAjye matim akurmahi
09003015c te saMtyajya tanUr yAtAH zUrA brahmavidAM gatim
09003016a vayaM tv iha vinAbhUtA guNavadbhir mahArathaiH
09003016c kRpaNaM vartayiSyAma pAtayitvA nRpAn bahUn
09003017a sarvair api ca jIvadbhir bIbhatsur aparAjitaH
09003017c kRSNanetro mahAbAhur devair api durAsadaH
09003018a indrakArmukavajrAbham indraketum ivocchritam
09003018c vAnaraM ketum AsAdya saMcacAla mahAcamUH
09003019a siMhanAdena bhImasya pAJcajanyasvanena ca
09003019c gANDIvasya ca nirghoSAt saMhRSyanti manAMsi naH
09003020a carantIva mahAvidyun muSNantI nayanaprabhAm
09003020c alAtam iva cAviddhaM gANDIvaM samadRzyata
09003021a jAmbUnadavicitraM ca dhUyamAnaM mahad dhanuH
09003021c dRzyate dikSu sarvAsu vidyud abhraghaneSv iva
09003022a uhyamAnaz ca kRSNena vAyuneva balAhakaH
09003022c tAvakaM tad balaM rAjann arjuno 'stravidAM varaH
09003022e gahanaM zizire kakSaM dadAhAgnir ivotthitaH
09003023a gAhamAnam anIkAni mahendrasadRzaprabham
09003023c dhanaMjayam apazyAma caturdantam iva dvipam
09003024a vikSobhayantaM senAM te trAsayantaM ca pArthivAn
09003024c dhanaMjayam apazyAma nalinIm iva kuJjaram
09003025a trAsayantaM tathA yodhAn dhanur ghoSeNa pANDavam
09003025c bhUya enam apazyAma siMhaM mRgagaNA iva
09003026a sarvalokamaheSvAsau vRSabhau sarvadhanvinAm
09003026c Amuktakavacau kRSNau lokamadhye virejatuH
